#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अवं॑त॒मत्र॑ये गृ॒हं कृ॑णु॒तं यु॒वम॑श्विना ।  
> अंति॒ षद्भू॑तु वा॒मवः॑ ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अवंतम् । अत्रये । गृहम् । कृणुतम् । युवम् । अश्विना ।  
> अंति । सत् । भूतु । वाम् । अवः ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अश्विना युवं युवाम् अत्रये अग्न्यागारे दह्यमानाय अवंतं रक्षंतं गृहं कृणुतं कृतवंतौ । तादृशयोः वामवः भवतु । अवंतमिति व्यत्ययेन पुँल्लिंगता ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Aśvins, you two made for Atri, who was being burnt in the house of fire, a protecting (अवंतं (*avaṃtaṃ*)) house (गृहं (*gṛhaṃ*)). Let the protection of such as you be near. The word अवंतं (*avaṃtaṃ*) is masculine by a change of gender.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अश्विना (*aśvinā*)—O Aśvins
- युवं (*yuvaṃ*)—you two
- अत्रये (*atraye*)—for Atri, who had been thrown into the fire
- अवंतं (*avaṃtaṃ*)—protecting
- गृहं (*gṛhaṃ*)—a house
- कृणुतं (*kṛṇutaṃ*)—you made
- वां (*vāṃ*)—your
- अवः (*avaḥ*)—protection
- अंतिषत् (*aṃtiṣat*)—staying very close to us
- भूतु (*bhūtu*)—let it be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, you two made a protecting house for Atri, who had been thrown into the fire by the Asuras. Grant that your protection may be very close to us.

##### English translation
Aswins, you made a sheltering house for Atri let your protection abide near me.

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वरे॑थे अ॒ग्निमा॒तपो॒ वद॑ते व॒ल्ग्वत्र॑ये ।  
> अंति॒ षद्भू॑तु वा॒मवः॑ ॥ ८ ॥

### Printed p. 105

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वरेथे इति । अग्निम् । आऽतपः । वदते । वल्गु । अत्रये ।  
> अंति । सत् । भूतु । वाम् । अवः ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अश्विनौ वल्गु मनोहरं वदते स्तुवते अत्रये आतपः आतपादौष्ण्यात् अग्निं वरेथे आवारयतम् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Aśvins, for Atri, who praises you (वदते (*vadate*)) charmingly (वल्गु (*valgu*)), you two kept off (वरेथे (*varethe*)) the fire from the heat of its burning (आतपः (*ātapaḥ*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Aśvins) वल्गु (*valgu*)—charmingly
- वदते (*vadate*)—who praised
- अत्रये (*atraye*)—for Atri
- अतपः (*atapaḥ*)—from its burning fierceness
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—the fire
- वरेथे (*varethe*)—you held back
- वां (*vāṃ*)—your
- अवः (*avaḥ*)—protection
- अंतिषत् (*aṃtiṣat*)—staying very close
- भूतु (*bhūtu*)—let it be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, for Atri, who praised you charmingly, you held back the fire from its burning fierceness. Grant that your protection may be very close to us.

##### English translation
You stayed the fire from its fierceness for Atri, while he praised you acceptably ; let your protection abide near me.

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्र स॒प्तव॑ध्रिरा॒शसा॒ धारा॑म॒ग्नेर॑शायत ।  
> अंति॒ षद्भू॑तु वा॒मवः॑ ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्र । सप्तऽवध्रिः । आऽशसा । धाराम् । अग्नेः । अशायत ।  
> अंति । सत् । भूतु । वाम् । अवः ॥ ९ ॥

### Printed p. 106

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सप्तवध्रिः महर्षिर्हे अश्विनौ युवयोः आशसा आशंसनेन स्तुत्या मंजूषाया निर्गत्य अग्नेः धारां तस्यां मंजूषायां प्र अशायत आशाययत् । स्वनिरोधिकां तां दग्धवानित्यर्थः । सप्तवध्रेः पेटिकांतःप्रवेशोऽश्विनोरनुग्रहान्निर्गमश्च ‘वि जिहीष्व वनस्पते' (ऋ. सं. ५. ७८.५) इत्यत्र स्पष्टमुक्तम् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The great seer Saptavadhri, O Aśvins, by praise (आशसा (*āśasā*)) of you, came out of the chest and placed (प्र अशायत (*pra aśāyata*)) the stream (धारां (*dhārāṃ*)) of Agni in that chest; that is, he burnt the chest that confined him. Saptavadhri's entering the chest and coming out through the grace of the Aśvins is stated clearly in the passage वि जिहीष्व वनस्पते (*vi jihīṣva vanaspate*) (Ṛ. Saṃ. 5. 78.5). **GLOSS**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Aśvins) सप्तवध्रिः (*saptavadhriḥ*)—the seer named Saptavadhri
- आशसा (*āśasā*)—by the praise that extols you (coming out of the chest in which he had hidden himself)
- अग्नेः (*agneḥ*)—of Agni
- धारां (*dhārāṃ*)—the flame
- प्र अशायत (*pra aśāyata*)—placed it in that chest and made it burn it
- वां (*vāṃ*)—your
- अवः (*avaḥ*)—protection
- अंतिषत् (*aṃtiṣat*)—staying very close to us
- भूतु (*bhūtu*)—let it be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, the seer named Saptavadhri praised you and, as a result of your help, came out of the chest in which he had hidden himself, and made that chest burn with the fire. May your protection always be very close to us.

##### English translation
Through his praise of you Saptavadhri let the fire's flame-point (to his basket) ; let your protection abide near me.

#### Ṛk 10

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इ॒हा ग॑तं वृषण्वसू शृणु॒तं म॑ इ॒मं हव॑म् ।  
> अंति॒ षद्भू॑तु वा॒मवः॑ ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इह । आ । गतम् । वृषण्वसू इति वृषण्ऽवसू । शृणुतम् । मे । इमम् । हवम् ।  
> अंति । सत् । भूतु । वाम् । अवः ॥ १० ॥

### Printed p. 107

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे वृषण्वसू वर्षणधनावश्विनौ इह अस्मिन् यज्ञे आ गतम् आगच्छतम् । तदर्थं मे मम इमं हवं शृणुतम् ॥ ॥ १९ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Aśvins, you who are lords of showering wealth (वृषण्वसू (*vṛṣaṇvasū*)), come here to this sacrifice. For that, hear this invocation of mine.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वृषण्वसू (*vṛṣaṇvasū*)—O Aśvins, who shower on us the objects of our desire
- इह (*iha*)—to this our sacrifice
- आ गतं (*ā gataṃ*)—come
- मे (*me*)—my
- इमुं हवं (*imuṃ havaṃ*)—this prayer
- शृणुतं (*śṛṇutaṃ*)—hear
- वां (*vāṃ*)—your
- अवः (*avaḥ*)—protection
- अंतिषत् (*aṃtiṣat*)—staying very close to us
- भूतु (*bhūtu*)—let it be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, who fulfil all our desires, come to this our sacrifice. Hear this prayer of mine. May your protection be very close to us.

##### English translation
Come hither, lords of abundant wealth, hear this my invocation ; let your protection abide near me.

#### Ṛk 11

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> किमि॒दं वां॑ पुराण॒वज्जर॑तोरिव शस्यते ।  
> अंति॒ षद्भू॑तु वा॒मवः॑ ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> किम् । इदम् । वाम् । पुराणऽवत् । जरतोःऽइव । शस्यते ।  
> अंति । सत् । भूतु । वाम् । अवः ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अश्विनौ वां युवयोरर्थमागमनाय पुराणवत् पुराणयोरतिवृद्धयोरिव । तदेवाह । जरतोरिव शस्यते । पुनःपुनरागच्छतमिति शस्यते । किमिदम् । यथा लोके वृद्धो जीर्णो बहुवारमाहूतोऽपि नागच्छति तद्वद्युवामपीत्यर्थः । एवमनागमाद्ब्रवीति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Aśvins, for your coming, as if for two very old persons (पुराणवत् (*purāṇavat*)), as if for two aged ones (जरतोरिव (*jaratoriva*)), it is recited (शस्यते (*śasyate*)): "Come again and again." What is this? As in the world an old, decrepit man does not come though called many times, so you also do not; that is the meaning. Thus he speaks because of their not coming.

### Printed p. 108

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Aśvins) वां (*vāṃ*)—this praise addressed to you
- पुराणवत् (*purāṇavat*)—born in very ancient times
- जरतोरिव (*jaratoriva*)—as with old men, whose ears have become deaf, so that one has to say it again and again
- शस्यते (*śasyate*)—is being recited again and again
- किं इदं (*kiṃ idaṃ*)—what is the reason for this?
- वां (*vāṃ*)—your
- अवः (*avaḥ*)—protection
- अंतिषत् (*aṃtiṣat*)—staying very close
- भूतु (*bhūtu*)—let it be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, as with old men born in former times, who, if they are deaf, do not hear however many times one speaks to them, so I too offer my praise to you again and again, and yet it has not reached your ears. What is the reason for this? Grant that your protection may always be near us.

##### English translation
Why is this (repeated invocation) addresred to you as if you were decrepit like old men ? Let your protection abide near me.

#### Ṛk 12

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स॒मा॒नं वां॑ सजा॒त्यं॑ समा॒नो बंधु॑रश्विना ।  
> अंति॒ षद्भू॑तु वा॒मवः॑ ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> समानम् । वाम् । सऽजात्यम् । समानः । बंधुः । अश्विना ।  
> अंति । सत् । भूतु । वाम् । अवः ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अश्विना अश्विनौ वां युवयोः परस्परं सजात्यं समानजातित्वं समानम् एकमेव । उभयोरप्यश्व1रूपायाः सूर्यपत्न्या उत्पत्तेः सजात्यम् । तथा युवयोः बंधुः बंधकः स्रुवः समानः एक एव । अथवर्षिरहं समान एक एव बंधुः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Aśvins, the kinship of birth (सजात्यं (*sajātyaṃ*)) between the two of you is common (समानम् (*samānam*)), one only; for both of you the common birth is from the wife of Sūrya in the form of a mare. Likewise the binder (बंधुः (*baṃdhuḥ*)) of you two is common, one only. **NOTE**

### Printed p. 109

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अश्विना (*aśvinā*)—O Aśvins
- वां (*vāṃ*)—of you two
- सजात्यं (*sajātyaṃ*)—the family relationship
- समानं (*samānaṃ*)—is one and the same
- बंधुः (*baṃdhuḥ*)—the kinship also
- समानः (*samānaḥ*)—is one and the same
- वां (*vāṃ*)—your
- अवः (*avaḥ*)—protection
- अंतिषत् (*aṃtiṣat*)—staying very close to me
- भूत् (*bhūt*)—let it be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, the family relationship of you two is one and the same. Your kinship is also the same. (Therefore I belong to both of you in the same way.) Grant that your protection may be very close to me.

##### English translation
Aswins, your relationship is common and you have a common kinsman ; let your protection abide near me

#### Ṛk 13

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यो वां॒ रजां॑स्यश्विना॒ रथो॑ वि॒याति॒ रोद॑सी ।  
> अंति॒ षद्भू॑तु वा॒मवः॑ ॥ १३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । वाम् । रजांसि । अश्विना । रथः । विऽयाति । रोदसी इति ।  
> अंति । सत् । भूतु । वाम् । अवः ॥ १३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वां युवयोः यः रथः अस्ति स रथः रजांसि लोकान् रोदसी द्यावापृथिव्यौ च वियाति विशेषेण गच्छति । अतस्तेन रथेन शीघ्रमागच्छतमिति शेषः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The car (रथः (*rathaḥ*)) which is yours moves specially (वियाति (*viyāti*)) through the worlds (रजांसि (*rajāṃsi*)) and through heaven and earth (रोदसी (*rodasī*)). Therefore come quickly with that car; this is the part to be supplied.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अश्विना (*aśvinā*)—O Aśvins
- वां (*vāṃ*)—your
- यः (*yaḥ*)—which famous
- रथः (*rathaḥ*)—car there is
- रजांसि (*rajāṃsi*)—all the worlds
- रोदसी (*rodasī*)—heaven and earth

### Printed p. 110
- वियाति (*viyāti*)—it moves round exceedingly
- वां (*vāṃ*)—your
- अवः (*avaḥ*)—protection
- अंतिषत् (*aṃtiṣat*)—staying very close to us
- भूतु (*bhūtu*)—let it be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, your famous car moves exceedingly through all the worlds and through heaven and earth. May your protection always be close to us.

##### English translation
Your chariot, Aswins, moves swiftly through the worlds, through heaven and earth ; let your protection abide near me.

#### Ṛk 14

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ नो॒ गव्ये॑भि॒रश्व्यैः॑ स॒हस्रै॒रुप॑ गच्छतम् ।  
> अंति॒ षद्भू॑तु वा॒मवः॑ ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । नः । गव्येभिः । अश्व्यैः । सहस्रैः । उप । गच्छतम् ।  
> अंति । सत् । भूतु । वाम् । अवः ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अश्विनौ नः अस्मान् सहस्रैः अपरिमितैः गव्येभिः गोसमूहैः अश्व्यैः अश्वसमूहैश्च उप गच्छतम् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Aśvins, come to us (नः (*naḥ*)) with thousands (सहस्रैः (*sahasraiḥ*)), that is with countless herds of cattle (गव्येभिः (*gavyebhiḥ*)) and herds of horses (अश्व्यैः (*aśvyaiḥ*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Aśvins) नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- सहस्रैः (*sahasraiḥ*)—countless
- गव्येभिः (*gavyebhiḥ*)—with herds of cattle
- अश्वैः (*aśvaiḥ*)—with herds of horses
- उप आ गच्छतं (*upa ā gacchataṃ*)—graciously come
- वां (*vāṃ*)—your
- अवः (*avaḥ*)—protection
- अंतिषत् (*aṃtiṣat*)—staying very close
- भूतु (*bhūtu*)—let it be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, graciously come to us with countless herds of cattle and herds of horses. May your protection always be very close to us.

### Printed p. 111

##### English translation
Come to us with thousands of herds of cattle and horses; let your protection abide near me.

#### Ṛk 15

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> मा नो॒ गव्ये॑भि॒रश्व्यैः॑ स॒हस्रे॑भि॒रति॑ ख्यतम् ।  
> अंति॒ षद्भू॑तु वा॒मवः॑ ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> मा । नः । गव्येभिः । अश्व्यैः । सहस्रेभिः । अति । ख्यतम् ।  
> अंति । सत् । भूतु । वाम् । अवः ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अश्विनौ गव्येभिः गोसमूहैः अश्व्यैः अश्वसमूहैः सहस्रेभिः सहस्रसंख्याकैः मा अस्मान् अति ख्यतम् । अतीति प्रतीत्यस्मिन्नर्थे । मा निवारयतमित्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Aśvins, do not pass us by (अति ख्यतम् (*ati khyatam*)) with thousands (सहस्रेभिः (*sahasrebhiḥ*)), that is with herds of cattle (गव्येभिः (*gavyebhiḥ*)) and herds of horses (अश्व्यैः (*aśvyaiḥ*)); the meaning is: do not hinder us.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Aśvins) सहस्रेभिः (*sahasrebhiḥ*)—countless
- गव्येभिः (*gavyebhiḥ*)—with herds of cattle
- अश्वैः (*aśvaiḥ*)—and with herds of horses, you who are accompanied
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- मा अति ख्यतं (*mā ati khyataṃ*)—do not go past us (neglecting us)
- वां (*vāṃ*)—your
- अवः (*avaḥ*)—protection
- अंतिषत् (*aṃtiṣat*)—staying very close to us
- भूतु (*bhūtu*)—let it be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, you who are accompanied by countless herds of cattle and herds of horses, do not pass us by without regarding us. May your protection always be very close to me.

##### English translation
Pass us not by with your thousands of heards of cattle and horses ; let your protection abide near me.

### Printed p. 112
#### Ṛk 16

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒रु॒णप्सु॑रु॒षा अ॑भू॒दक॒र्ज्योति॑रृ॒ताव॑री ।  
> अंति॒ षद्भू॑तु वा॒मवः॑ ॥ १६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अरुणऽप्सुः । उषाः । अभूत् । अकः । ज्योतिः । ऋतऽवरी ।  
> अंति । सत् । भूतु । वाम् । अवः ॥ १६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अश्विनौ उषाः अरुणप्सुः शुभ्रवर्णा अभूत् भवति । न केवलं स्वयम् । ज्योतिः तेजः अकः करोति अंति सर्वतः ऋतावरी ऋतवत्युषाः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Aśvins, Uṣas has become bright-hued (अरुणप्सुः (*aruṇapsuḥ*)). Not only herself: she makes (अकः (*akaḥ*)) light (ज्योतिः (*jyotiḥ*)) on every side (अंति (*aṃti*)); Uṣas is possessed of ṛta (ऋतावरी (*ṛtāvarī*)).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Aśvins) अरुणप्सुः (*aruṇapsuḥ*)—of bright hue
- उषाः (*uṣāḥ*)—the goddess Uṣas
- अभूत् (*abhūt*)—has appeared
- ऋतावरी (*ṛtāvarī*)—Uṣas, who is chief for the sacrifice
- ज्योतिः (*jyotiḥ*)—her radiance (everywhere)
- अकः (*akaḥ*)—makes (spreads)
- वां (*vāṃ*)—your
- अवः (*avaḥ*)—protection
- अंतिषत् (*aṃtiṣat*)—staying very close to us
- भूतु (*bhūtu*)—let it be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, the bright-hued goddess Uṣas has appeared. Uṣas, who is chief for the sacrifice, has cast her radiance everywhere. O Aśvins, grant that your protection may be very close to us.

##### English translation
The purple-tinted Dawn has appeared, the mistress of the sacrifice spreads her light ; let your protection abide near me.

#### Ṛk 17

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒श्विना॒ सु वि॒चाक॑शद्वृ॒क्षं प॑रशु॒माँ इ॑व ।  
> अंति॒ षद्भू॑तु वा॒मवः॑ ॥ १७ ॥

### Printed p. 113

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अश्विना । सु । विऽचाकशत् । वृक्षम् । परशुमान्ऽइव ।  
> अंति । सत् । भूतु । वाम् । अवः ॥ १७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सु विचाकशत् अत्यंतं दीप्यमानः सूर्योऽग्निर्वा वृक्षं परशुमानिव स यथा शकलयति तद्वत्तमो निशारयतीति शेषः । दृष्टांतसामर्थ्यादेवं लभ्यते । यस्मादेवं तस्मात् अश्विना अश्विनावाह्वय इति शेषः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sun or Agni, shining exceedingly (सु विचाकशत् (*su vicākaśat*)), as a man with an axe (परशुमान् (*paraśumān*)) splits a tree (वृक्षं (*vṛkṣaṃ*)), so he drives away the darkness; this is the part to be supplied. This is understood from the force of the simile. Since it is so, "I invoke the Aśvins" is the part to be supplied.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Aśvins) परशुमान् (*paraśumān*)—the woodcutter who holds an axe
- वृक्षं इव (*vṛkṣaṃ iva*)—as he cuts a tree to pieces and splits it
- सु विचाकशत् (*su vicākaśat*)—the shining sun (or Agni) scatters the darkness
- वां (*vāṃ*)—your
- अवः (*avaḥ*)—protection
- अंति षत् (*aṃti ṣat*)—staying very close to us
- भूतु (*bhūtu*)—let it be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, as a woodcutter who holds an axe cuts a tree to pieces and splits it, so the shining sun, or Agni, scatters the darkness (the black gloom). Grant that your protection may always be very close to us.

##### English translation
Aswins, the splendidly-brilliant (sun cleaves the darkness) as the woodman with his axe a tree ; let your protection abide near me.

#### Ṛk 18

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> पुरं॒ न धृ॑ष्ण॒वा रु॑ज कृ॒ष्णया॑ बाधि॒तो वि॒शा ।  
> अंति॒ षद्भू॑तु वा॒मवः॑ ॥ १८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> पुरम् । न । धृष्णो इति । आ । रुज । कृष्णया । बाधितः । विशा ।  
> अंति । सत् । भूतु । वाम् । अवः ॥ १८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

### Printed p. 114

> हे धृष्णो धर्षक सप्तवध्रे त्वं कृष्णया आकर्षया विशा प्रवेशयंत्या पेटिकया बाधितः त्वं ततो निर्गत्य तामेव आ रुज पीडय अश्विनोरनुग्रहात् । एवं स्वयं स्वात्मानं प्रेष्यति । अथवा गोपवनः सप्तवध्रिमेवं ब्रवीति । वां युवयोः अवः रक्षणं गमनं वा समीपे । तत्र त्रिषु वर्गेषु  अंति षत्' इत्युत्तरोऽर्धर्चोऽन्वितपदाध्याहारेण योज्यः ॥ ॥ २० ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O bold (धृष्णो (*dhṛṣṇo*)) Saptavadhri, you, afflicted (बाधितः (*bādhitaḥ*)) by the dark (कृष्णया (*kṛṣṇayā*)) chest (पेटिकया (*peṭikayā*)) that draws in and encloses (विशा (*viśā*)), come out of it and break (आ रुज (*ā ruja*)) that very chest, by the favour of the Aśvins. So he exhorts himself. Or else Gopavana speaks thus to Saptavadhri. Your (वां (*vāṃ*)) protection (अवः (*avaḥ*)) or going is near. In the three vargas the latter half-Ṛk अंति षत् (*aṃti ṣat*) is to be connected by supplying a fitting word.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- धृष्णो (*dhṛṣṇo*)—O Saptavadhri, who have firm strength
- कृष्णया (*kṛṣṇayā*)—that binds
- विशा (*viśā*)—by the chest
- बाधितः (*bādhitaḥ*)—you, who are harmed
- पुरं न (*puraṃ na*)—as one splits an enemy's city and comes out
- आ रुज (*ā ruja*)—split that chest and come out (O Aśvins)
- वां (*vāṃ*)—your
- अवः (*avaḥ*)—protection
- अंतिषत् (*aṃtiṣat*)—staying very close to us
- भूतु (*bhūtu*)—let it be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Saptavadhri, who have firm strength, harmed by the chest that bound you, split that chest and come out, as one splits an enemy's city and comes out. O Aśvins, may your protection be very close to me.

##### English translation
O bold Saptavadhri, distressed by the entangling and detaining (basket), break through it as through a city ; let your protection ( Aswins ) abide near me.

## Sūkta 74 (Maṇḍala 8)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ‘विशोविशो वः' इति पंचदशर्चं पंचमं सूक्तम् । अत्रेयमनुक्रमणिका -–• विशोविशो वः पंचोनाग्नेयं त्वनुष्टुम्मुखास्तृचाश्चत्वारोऽंत्यास्तिस्रोऽनुष्टुभ आर्क्षस्य श्रुतर्वणो दानस्तुतिः' इति । अनुवृत्तेर्गोपवन ऋषिः । आदितस्त्रिषु तृचेषु सर्वाः प्रथमा अनुष्टुभो द्वितीयातृतीये प्राग्वत्सप्रपरिभाषया गायत्र्यौ त्रयोदश्याद्यास्तिस्रोऽनुष्टुभः । अस्योत्तरस्य चाग्निर्देवता । अंत्यास्तिस्रः श्रुतर्वनाम्नो राज्ञो दानस्तुतिः । व्योमविश्वदेवस्तुत्पंचशारदीयेष्विदं सूक्तमाज्यशस्त्रम् । सूत्रितं च-’ विशोविशो वो अतिथिमित्याज्यम्' (आश्व. श्रौ. ९. ८) इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning विशोविशो वः (*viśoviśo vaḥ*) has fifteen Ṛks and is the fifth sūkta. The Anukramaṇikā says of it that it is addressed to Agni, that four tṛcas begin with an Anuṣṭubh and that the last three Ṛks are Anuṣṭubh, and that it is a praise of the gift of Śrutarvan, son of Ṛkṣa. Gopavana is the seer, by continuation. In the three tṛcas from the beginning all the first Ṛks are Anuṣṭubh and the second and third are Gāyatrī, as before; the thirteenth and the two following are Anuṣṭubh. The deity of this and of the following is Agni. The last three are a praise of the gift of the king named Śrutarvan. This sūkta is the Ājya-śastra in certain rites; the use is set out in the sūtra विशोविशो वो अतिथिमित्याज्यम् (*viśoviśo vo atithimityājyam*) (Āśv. Śrau. 9. 8). **NOTE**

### Printed p. 115

> श्रुतर्वनाम्नो राज्ञो दानस्तुतिः ॥ व्योमविश्वदेवस्त्वंचशारदीयेऽस्मिदं सूक्त-माज्यशस्त्रं । सूत्रितं च । विशोविशो वो अतिथिमित्याज्यं । आ. ९-८ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The last three Ṛks are the praise of the gift of the king named Śrutarvan. In the rites called Vyoma, Viśvadeva and the five Śāradīya rites this sūkta is the Ājya-śastra; the use is set out in the sūtra विशोविशो वो अतिथिमित्याज्यं (*viśoviśo vo atithimityājyaṃ*) (Ā. 9-8). **NOTE**

Translation of the Kannada: This sūkta beginning विशोविशो वः (*viśoviśo vaḥ*) is the fifth sūkta in the eighth Maṇḍala. It has fifteen Ṛks. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said: विशोविशो वः पंचोनाग्नेयं त्वनुष्टुबुम्मखास्त्र्यचाश्चत्वारोऽन्त्यास्तिस्रोऽनुष्टुभ आर्क्षस्य श्रुतर्वणो दानस्तुतिः (*viśoviśo vaḥ paṃconāgneyaṃ tvanuṣṭubummakhāstryacāścatvāro 'ntyāstisro 'nuṣṭubha ārkṣasya śrutarvaṇo dānastutiḥ*). The seer of this sūkta is the one named Gopavana. Of the first three tṛcas of this sūkta (a tṛca is three Ṛks) the first Ṛk is Anuṣṭubh, and the second and third Ṛks are in the Gāyatrī metre. The three Ṛks from the thirteenth onwards are in the Anuṣṭubh metre. Agni is the deity of this sūkta and of the next sūkta. For the last three Ṛks of this sūkta the praise of the gift of the king named Śrutarvā is the deity. It is to be used for the Ājya-śastra mantras in the Vyoma, Viśvadeva and Pañcaśāradīya rites, as it is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta-sūtra विशोविशो वोः अथितिमिथ्याज्यं (*viśoviśo voḥ athitimithyājyaṃ*) (Ā. 9-8). **NOTE**

Sūkta 24. Maṇḍala 8, Anuvāka 8, Sūkta 24. Aṣṭaka 6, Adhyāya 5, Vargas 21, 22, 23. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 15. Seer: Gopavana Ātreya. Deity: 1-12 Agni; 13-15 the praise of the gift of Śrutarvan Ārkṣa. Metre: 1, 4, 7, 10, 13-15 Anuṣṭubh; 2, 3, 5, 6, 8, 9, 11, 12 Gāyatrī.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वि॒शोवि॑शो वो॒ अति॑थिं वाज॒यंतः॑ पुरुप्रि॒यम् ।  
> अ॒ग्निं वो॒ दुर्यं॒ वच॑ स्तु॒षे शू॒षस्य॒ मन्म॑भिः ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> विशःऽविशः । वः । अतिथिम् । वाजऽयंतः । पुरुऽप्रियम् ।  
> अग्निम् । वः । दुर्यम् । वचः । स्तुषे । शूषस्य । मन्मऽभिः ॥ १ ॥

### Printed p. 116

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अन्य ऋत्विजो यजमानाश्च वः यूयं वाजयंतः अन्नमिच्छंतः विशोविशः सर्वस्याः प्रजायाः अतिथिं पूज्यं पुरुप्रियं बहुप्रियम् अग्निं स्तुत्या परिचरतेति शेषः । अहं च वः युष्मदर्थं दुर्यं गुहा हितं वचः स्तुषे शूषस्य सुखस्य लाभाय । कैः साधनैः । मन्मभिः मननीयैः स्तोत्रैः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

He addresses the other priests and sacrificers: you, desiring food, serve with praise Agni, the guest, the one to be honoured, of every people, beloved of many. And I, for you, praise the homely speech, for the gain of happiness, with what means: with hymns worthy of reflection.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वः (*vaḥ*)—you (O priests)
- वाजयंतः (*vājayaṃtaḥ*)—desirous of food
- विशोविशः (*viśoviśaḥ*)—for every human people
- अतिथिं (*atithiṃ*)—the one to be honoured
- पुरुप्रियं (*purupriyaṃ*)—the one dear to many
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—serve Agni
- वः (*vaḥ*)—for you
- शूषस्य (*śūṣasya*)—for happiness
- मन्मभिः (*manmabhiḥ*)—with hymns of meditative nature
- दुर्यं (*duryaṃ*)—relating to the house
- वचः (*vacaḥ*)—the speech of praise
- स्तुषे (*stuṣe*)—I recite

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O priests, you, desiring food, serve Agni, who is to be honoured by every human people and is dear to many. For your happiness, with hymns of meditative nature, I recite the speech of praise relating to the house.

##### English translation
Food-desiring (priests, worship) Agni, who is the guest of all man kind, beloved of many ; I address to him in your behalf a domestic homage with hymns, for the attainment of happiness ;

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यं जना॑सो ह॒विष्मं॑तो मि॒त्रं न स॒र्पिरा॑सुतिम् ।  
> प्र॒शंसं॑ति॒ प्रश॑स्तिभिः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यम् । जनासः । हविष्मंतः । मित्रम् । न । सर्पिःऽआसुतिम् ।  
> प्रऽशंसंति । प्रशस्तिऽभिः ॥ २ ॥

### Printed p. 117

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यम् अग्निं जनासः जना यजमानाः हविष्मंतः संतः मित्रं न मित्रमिवादित्यमिव सखायमिव वा सर्पिरासुतिं सर्पिरासूयते हूयते यस्मिंस्तादृशं प्रशंसंति स्तुवंति प्रशस्तिभिः स्तुतिभिः स्तुव इति शेषः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The Agni whom the people, the sacrificers, having oblations, praise with praises, like a friend, that is, like Mitra (the sun) or like a companion, in whom clarified butter is poured and offered; I praise him, the verb being understood.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मित्रं न (*mitraṃ na*)—as one serves a friend
- हविष्मंतः (*haviṣmaṃtaḥ*)—possessing oblations
- जनासः (*janāsaḥ*)—the sacrificers
- सर्पिरासुतिं (*sarpirāsutiṃ*)—having oblations offered with praises
- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Agni
- प्रशस्तिभिः (*praśastibhiḥ*)—with words of praise
- प्रशंसंति (*praśaṃsaṃti*)—they praise (that Agni I praise)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The sacrificers, possessing oblations, praise and honour with words of praise, as they serve their friends, that Agni who has the oblations offered with praises; that Agni I too praise.

##### English translation
(That Agni), to whom clarified butter is offered, whom men, bearing oblations, worship with praises as a friend ;

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> पन्यां॑सं जा॒तवे॑दसं॒ यो दे॒वता॒त्युद्य॑ता ।  
> ह॒व्यान्यैर॑यद्दि॒वि ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> पन्यांसम् । जातऽवेदसम् । यः । देवऽताति । उत्ऽयता ।  
> हव्यानि । ऐरयत् । दिवि ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पन्यांसम् अतिशयेन स्तोतारं साधु कृतमिति यजमानं स्तुवंतं जातवेदसं जातधनं जातविद्यं वा स्तुव इति शेषः । यः अग्निः देवताति देवतातौ यज्ञे उद्यता उद्यतानि हव्यानि हवींषि दिवि ऐरयत् प्रेरयति दिवि देवेभ्यः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

पन्यांसं (*panyāṃsaṃ*) is the greatest praiser, one who praises the sacrificer, saying "well done"; जातवेदसं (*jātavedasaṃ*) is one in whom wealth is born, or one by whom knowledge is born; I praise him, the verb being understood. The Agni who, in the sacrifice (देवताति (*devatāti*)), sends the oblations that are offered up to heaven, for the gods.

### Printed p. 118

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Agni
- देवताति (*devatāti*)—in the sacrifice addressed to the gods
- उद्यता (*udyatā*)—offered
- हव्यानि (*havyāni*)—the oblations
- दिवि (*divi*)—to the world of the gods
- ऐरयत् (*airayat*)—sends and dispatches, such a one
- पन्यांसं (*panyāṃsaṃ*)—one who praises the sacrificer who worships him
- जातवेदसं (*jātavedasaṃ*)—and the one who knows everything, that Agni I praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The Agni who carries to the world of the gods the oblations offered in the sacrifice addressed to the gods, who praises the sacrificer who worships him, and who knows everything, that Agni I praise.

##### English translation
Jatavedas, the earnest praiser of his worshipper, who sends to heaven the oblations presented in the sacrifice.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आग॑न्म वृत्र॒हंत॑मं॒ ज्येष्ठ॑म॒ग्निमान॑वम् ।  
> यस्य॑ श्रु॒तर्वा॑ बृ॒हन्ना॒र्क्षो अनी॑क॒ एध॑ते ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । अगन्म । वृत्रहन्ऽतमम् । ज्येष्ठम् । अग्निम् । आनवम् ।  
> यस्य । श्रुतर्वा । बृहन् । आर्क्षः । अनीके । एधते ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वृत्रहंतमं पापानामतिशयेन हंतारं ज्येष्ठं प्रशस्यम् आनवं मनुष्यसंबंधिनं तेषां हितकारिणम् अग्निम् आगन्म आगता वयम् । पूजार्थं बहुवचनम् । यस्य अग्नेः अनीके ज्वालासंघे बृहन् महान् आर्क्षः ऋक्षपुत्रः श्रुतर्वा नाम राजा एधते वर्धते । कर्म करोतीत्यर्थः । तमग्निमागन्मेति समन्वयः । एवं श्रुतर्वाणं भिक्षयागतो गोपवनोऽग्निं स्तौति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

We have come to Agni, the greatest destroyer of the sins, the most excellent, the one belonging to men (आनवं (*ānavaṃ*)), their benefactor; the plural is for honour. In the flame-host of which Agni the mighty Ārkṣa, son of Ṛkṣa, the king named Śrutarvā, thrives; that is, he performs the rite. So the connection is: we have come to that Agni. Thus Gopavana, who has come to Śrutarvā for alms, praises Agni.

### Printed p. 119

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यस्य (*yasya*)—of which Agni
- अनीके (*anīke*)—with the help of the army in the form of flames
- बृहन् (*bṛhan*)—endowed with greatness
- आर्क्षः (*ārkṣaḥ*)—the son of Ṛkṣa
- श्रुतर्वा (*śrutarvā*)—the king named Śrutarvan
- एधते (*edhate*)—thrives, such a one
- वृत्रहंतमं (*vṛtrahaṃtamaṃ*)—the destroyer of sinners
- ज्येष्ठं (*jyeṣṭhaṃ*)—the most worthy of praise
- आनवं (*ānavaṃ*)—the benefactor of men
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—to Agni
- आ अगन्म (*ā aganma*)—we have come

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

We have come near to Agni, the destroyer of sinners, the most worthy of praise, the benefactor of men, with the help of whose army in the form of flames the king, endowed with greatness, the son of Ṛkṣa, named Śrutarvan, thrives.

##### English translation
We have come to that most excellent Agni, mightiest destroyer of the wicked, the benefactor of men, in whose army (of rays) Srutarvan the mighty son of Riksha, waxes great.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒मृतं॑ जा॒तवे॑दसं ति॒रस्तमां॑सि दर्श॒तम् ।  
> घृ॒ताह॑वन॒मीड्य॑म् ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अमृतम् । जातऽवेदसम् । तिरः । तमांसि । दर्शतम् ।  
> घृतऽआहवनम् । ईड्यम् ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> स एवागत्य स्तौति । अमृतम् अमरणं जातवेदसं जाततेजआद्युपलक्षणधनं तमांसि तिरः दर्शतं नाशयंतमित्यर्थः। घृताहवनम् । घृतमाहूयते यत्र तम् । ईड्यं स्तुत्यम्। ईदृशमागन्मेति संबंधः ॥ ॥ २१ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The same, having come, praises: अमृतम् (*amṛtam*) is the one who does not die; जातवेदसं (*jātavedasaṃ*) is the one in whom wealth, marked by born brilliance and the like, is born; तमांसि तिरः दर्शतं (*tamāṃsi tiraḥ darśataṃ*) means the one who destroys the darkness. घृताहवनम् (*ghṛtāhavanam*) is the one in whom ghee is poured; ईड्यं (*īḍyaṃ*) is the one worthy of praise. The connection is: we have come to such a one.

### Printed p. 120

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अमृतं (*amṛtaṃ*)—the one free from the nature of death
- जातवेदसं (*jātavedasaṃ*)—the one who knows everything
- तमांसि (*tamāṃsi*)—the black darknesses
- तिरः दर्शतं (*tiraḥ darśataṃ*)—the one who destroys
- घृताहवनं (*ghṛtāhavanaṃ*)—the one to whom the oblation in the form of ghee is offered
- ईड्यं (*īḍyaṃ*)—the one to be honoured; we have approached such an Agni

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

We have approached Agni, who is free from the nature of death, who knows everything, who destroys the black darknesses, who is honoured with the oblation in the form of ghee, and who is worthy of praise.

##### English translation
(We have come) to the mortal Jatavedas, who shows light across the darkness, well worthy of praise, and  receiving the offerings of ghi ;

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स॒बाधो॒ यं जना॑ इ॒मे॒३॒॑ऽग्निं ह॒व्येभि॒रीळ॑ते ।  
> जुह्वा॑नासो य॒तस्रु॑चः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सऽबाधः । यम् । जनाः । इमे । अग्निम् । हव्येभिः । ईळते ।  
> जुह्वानासः । यतऽस्रुचः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> इमे सबाधः बाधसहिता अध्वर्य्वादयः यम् अग्निं हव्येभिः हविर्भिः ईळते स्तुवंति । कीदृशा जनाः। जुह्वानासः यागं कुर्वाणाः यतस्रुचः तदर्थं धृतस्रुग्दंडाः । तमागन्मेति समन्वयः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

These Adhvaryus and others, सबाधः (*sabādhaḥ*), together with obstruction, praise with oblations the Agni whom they praise. What kind of people: जुह्वानासः (*juhvānāsaḥ*), performing the sacrifice; यतस्रुचः (*yatasrucaḥ*), holding the ladle-handles for it. The connection is: we have come to him. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- जुह्वानासः (*juhvānāsaḥ*)—those who perform the sacrifice
- यतस्रुचः (*yatasrucaḥ*)—those who hold up the ladle full of ghee
- सबाधः (*sabādhaḥ*)—those who are gathered together
- इमे जनाः (*ime janāḥ*)—these Adhvaryus
- यं अग्निं (*yaṃ agniṃ*)—which Agni
- हव्येभिः (*havyebhiḥ*)—with oblations
- ईळते (*īḷate*)—they praise (we have approached that Agni)

### Printed p. 121

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

We have approached that Agni whom these Adhvaryus, who perform the sacrifice, who hold up the ladle full of ghee and who are gathered together, praise with oblations.

##### English translation
That Agni, whom these crowding worshippers honour with oblations offering to him with up-lifted ladles.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इ॒यं ते॒ नव्य॑सी म॒तिरग्ने॒ अधा॑य्य॒स्मदा ।  
> मंद्र॒ सुजा॑त॒ सुक्र॒तोऽमू॑र॒ दस्माति॑थे ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इयम् । ते । नव्यसी । मतिः । अग्ने । अधायि । अस्मत् । आ ।  
> मंद्र । सुऽजात । सुक्रतो इति सुऽक्रतो । अमूर । दस्म । अतिथे ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने इयम् इदानीं क्रियमाणा नव्यसी नवतरा स्तुतिः ते तव स्वभूता अस्मत् अस्मासु अधायि धृताभूत् । वयं तव स्तुतिं कुर्म इत्यर्थः । हे मंद्र मोदमान सुजात शोभनजनन सुक्रतो शोभनकर्मन् अमूर अमूढ दस्म दर्शनीय अतिथे अतिथिवत् पूज्य इत्यग्नेर्विशेषणानि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, this newest praise, now being made, belonging to you, has been set among us (अधायि (*adhāyi*)); the meaning is that we make praise of you. The words मंद्र (*maṃdra*) (rejoicing), सुजात (*sujāta*) (of good birth), सुक्रतो (*sukrato*) (of good works), अमूर (*amūra*) (not bewildered), दस्म (*dasma*) (beautiful to behold) and अतिथे (*atithe*) (to be honoured like a guest) are epithets of Agni.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मंद्र (*maṃdra*)—one who gives joy
- सुजात (*sujāta*)—one of excellent birth
- सुक्रतो (*sukrato*)—one of excellent works
- अमूर (*amūra*)—the wise one
- दस्म (*dasma*)—one of attractive form
- अतिथे (*atithe*)—the one to be honoured
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- ते (*te*)—addressed to you
- नव्यसी (*navyasī*)—new
- इयं मतिः (*iyaṃ matiḥ*)—this praise
- अस्मत् (*asmat*)—by us
- आ अधायि (*ā adhāyi*)—has been set for your praise

### Printed p. 122

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, who give joy, are of excellent birth, are the doer of excellent works, are wise, of attractive form, and to be honoured, this new praise addressed to you has been set by us for your praise (has been made).

##### English translation
This new hymn has been made by us for you, O joyful, well-born Agni, glorious in deeds, unbewildered, beautiful, the guest (of man);

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सा ते॑ अग्ने॒ शंत॑मा॒ चनि॑ष्ठा भवतु प्रि॒या ।  
> तया॑ वर्धस्व॒ सुष्टु॑तः ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सा । ते । अग्ने । शम्ऽतमा । चनिष्ठा । भवतु । प्रिया ।  
> तया । वर्धस्व । सुऽस्तुतः ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने सा अस्माभिः क्रियमाणा स्तुतिः शंतमा अत्यंतं सुखकरा चनिष्ठा अतिशयेनान्नवती ते तव प्रिया भवतु । तया स्तुत्या सुष्टुतः सुष्ठु स्तुतः सन् वर्धस्व प्रवृद्धो भव ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, that praise made by us, most pleasant (शंतमा (*śaṃtamā*)) and most rich in food (चनिष्ठा (*caniṣṭhā*)), let it be dear to you. Being well praised by that praise, grow, become greatly increased.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- सा (*sā*)—the praise made by us
- शंतमा (*śaṃtamā*)—most productive of happiness
- चनिष्ठा (*caniṣṭhā*)—endowed with abundant food
- ते (*te*)—to you
- प्रिया (*priyā*)—productive of what is dear
- भवतु (*bhavatu*)—let it be
- तया (*tayā*)—by that same praise
- सुष्टुतः (*suṣṭutaḥ*)—you, well praised
- वर्धस्व (*vardhasva*)—attain increase

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, let the praise made by us be most productive of happiness, endowed with abundant food and productive of what is dear to you. Well praised by that same praise, attain increase.

### Printed p. 123

##### English translation
Agni, may it be dear to you, most pleasant and most agreeable—well praised by it, do you wax great.

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सा द्यु॒म्नैर्द्यु॒म्निनी॑ बृ॒हदुपो॑प॒ श्रव॑सि॒ श्रवः॑ ।  
> दधी॑त वृत्र॒तूर्ये॑ ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सा । द्युम्नैः । द्युम्निनी । बृहत् । उपऽउप । श्रवसि । श्रवः ।  
> दधीत । वृत्रऽतूर्ये ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सा अस्माभिः क्रियमाणा स्तुतिः द्युम्नैः द्योतमानैरन्नैः अस्मभ्यं प्रदेयैः द्युम्निनी अन्नवती श्रवसि पूर्वस्मिन् विद्यमानेऽन्ने पुनरपि बृहत् महत् श्रवः अन्नम् उपोप दधीत पुनरुपरि धारयतु । कुत्रेति उच्यते । वृत्रतूर्ये संग्रामे । शत्रोः संबंधीति यावत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

That praise made by us, with shining foods (द्युम्नैः (*dyumnaiḥ*)) to be given to us, is rich in food (द्युम्निनी (*dyumninī*)); to the food (श्रवसि (*śravasi*)) that already exists, again may it add great food (श्रवः (*śravaḥ*)), and again and again place more. Where? It is said: in the battle (वृत्रतूर्ये (*vṛtratūrye*)), that is, the one connected with the enemy. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सा (*sā*)—this praise made and recited by us
- द्युम्नैः (*dyumnaiḥ*)—with splendid food-wealths
- द्युम्निनी (*dyumninī*)—the one that joins new food
- श्रवसि (*śravasi*)—with our food that is behind
- वृत्रतूर्ये (*vṛtratūrye*)—in battle, seized from enemies
- बृहत् (*bṛhat*)—abundant
- श्रवः (*śravaḥ*)—new food
- उपोप (*upopa*)—in great measure
- दधीत (*dadhīta*)—let it join and increase

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May this praise made and recited by us, which joins new food with splendid food-wealths, add abundantly and increase the new food, abundant and seized from enemies in battle, together with our food that is behind.

### Printed p. 124

##### English translation
May this (hymn) the rich source of wealth, heap  abundance o nour abundance (with stores won from our enemies) in battle.

#### Ṛk 10

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अश्व॒मिद्गां र॑थ॒प्रां त्वे॒षमिंद्रं॒ न सत्प॑तिम् ।  
> यस्य॒ श्रवां॑सि॒ तूर्व॑थ॒ पन्यं॑पन्यं च कृ॒ष्टयः॑ ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अश्वम् । इत् । गाम् । रथऽप्राम् । त्वेषम् । इंद्रम् । न । सत्ऽपतिम् ।  
> यस्य । श्रवांसि । तूर्वथ । पन्यम्ऽपन्यम् । च । कृष्टयः ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> गां गंतारम् अश्वमित् । इच्छब्द इवार्थे। अश्वमिव । तं यथा स्तुवते तथेत्यर्थः। रथप्रां रथानामस्मदीयानां पूरयितारं धनैः तथा त्वेषं दीप्तमग्निं सत्पतिं सतां पालकम् इंद्रं न इंद्रमिवेमं कृष्टयः मनुष्याः परिचरतेति शेषः । यस्य अग्नेर्बलेन श्रवांसि अन्नानि शत्रुसंबंधीनि तूर्वथ तथा पन्यंपन्यं च यद्यत् स्तुत्यं धनमस्ति तदपि तूर्वथ हिंस्थ ॥ ॥ २२ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

गां (*gāṃ*) means the one who goes; अश्वमित् (*aśvamit*): the word इत् (*it*) is in the sense of "like", that is, like a horse; as men praise him, so. रथप्रां (*rathaprāṃ*) is the one who fills our chariots with riches; likewise the shining (त्वेषं (*tveṣaṃ*)) Agni, the protector of the good (सत्पतिं (*satpatiṃ*)), like Indra: men (कृष्टयः (*kṛṣṭayaḥ*)), serve this one, the verb being understood. By whose strength you overcome (तूर्वथ (*tūrvatha*)) the foods (श्रवांसि (*śravāṃsi*)) belonging to the enemy, and likewise whatever praiseworthy wealth (पन्यंपन्यं (*panyaṃpanyaṃ*)) there is, that too you destroy.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- कृष्टयः (*kṛṣṭayaḥ*)—O men
- यस्य (*yasya*)—by the power of which Agni
- श्रवांसि (*śravāṃsi*)—the foods belonging to the enemy
- तूर्वथ (*tūrvatha*)—you seize and fill yourselves
- पन्यं पन्यं च (*panyaṃ panyaṃ ca*)—and the valuable riches too you seize and fill yourselves, such a one
- अश्वमित् (*aśvamit*)—like a horse
- गां (*gāṃ*)—the one who moves about
- रथप्रां (*rathaprāṃ*)—the one who fills our chariots with enemy wealth
- त्वेषं (*tveṣaṃ*)—the radiant one
- सत्पतिं (*satpatiṃ*)—the protector of good men
- इंद्रं न (*iṃdraṃ na*)—the one honoured like Indra, serve that Agni

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O men, serve that Agni, by whose power you seize and fill yourselves with the foods belonging to the enemy, and seize and fill yourselves with their valuable riches too, who moves about like a horse,

### Printed p. 125

who fills our chariots with the enemy's wealth, who is radiant, the protector of good men, and honoured like Indra.

##### English translation
(Worship), you men, the bright (Agni), who goes like a horse and fills our chariots (with spoil), who protects the good like Indra, and by whose might you ravage the stores ( of your enemies ) and all their wonderful (wealth).

#### Ṛk 11

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यं त्वा॑ गो॒पव॑नो गि॒रा चनि॑ष्ठदग्ने अंगिरः ।  
> स पा॑वक श्रुधी॒ हव॑म् ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यम् । त्वा । गोपवनः । गिरा । चनिष्ठत् । अग्ने । अंगिरः ।  
> सः । पावक । श्रुधि । हवम् ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने यं त्वा त्वां गोपवनः ऋषिः गिरा स्तुत्या चनिष्ठत् अतिशयेनान्नप्रदातारमकरोत् सः तादृशाग्ने अंगिरः सर्वत्र गंतरंगिरसां मध्य एक वा पावक शोधक हवं गोपवनस्य श्रुधि शृणु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, you whom the seer Gopavana by his praise has made the greatest giver of food, such a one, O Agni, अंगिरः (*aṃgiraḥ*), O purifier (पावक (*pāvaka*)), hear the call (हवं (*havaṃ*)) of Gopavana. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अंगिरः (*aṃgiraḥ*)—the one famed by the name Aṅgiras
- पावक (*pāvaka*)—the purifier
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- यं (*yaṃ*)—which
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- गोपवनः (*gopavanaḥ*)—the seer named Gopavana
- गिरा (*girā*)—by his praise
- चनिष्ठत् (*caniṣṭhat*)—made the excellent giver of food
- सः (*saḥ*)—such you
- हवं (*havaṃ*)—the praise of that Gopavana
- श्रुधि (*śrudhi*)—hear

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, famed by the name Aṅgiras and the purifier, you whom the seer named Gopavana has made by his praise the excellent giver of food, such as you, hear the praise of that Gopavana.

### Printed p. 126

##### English translation
Agni, Angiras, whom Gopavana by his praise has made the especial giver of food,—O purifier, hear his prayer.

#### Ṛk 12

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यं त्वा॒ जना॑स॒ ईळ॑ते स॒बाधो॒ वाज॑सातये ।  
> स बो॑धि वृत्र॒तूर्ये॑ ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यम् । त्वा । जनासः । ईळते । सऽबाधः । वाजऽसातये ।  
> सः । बोधि । वृत्रऽतूर्ये ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने यं त्वा त्वां जनासः जनाः स्तोतारो वा वाजसातये अन्नस्य लाभाय सबाधः निर्बंधरूपबाधोपेताः संतः ईळते स्तुवंति सः त्वं वृत्रतूर्ये वैरिनाशनाय पापक्षयाय वा बोधि बुध्यस्व । अथवा वृत्रतूर्ये संग्रामे बोधि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, you whom the people, or the praisers, for the gain of food (वाजसातये (*vājasātaye*)), being (सबाधः (*sabādhaḥ*)) afflicted by the obstruction of compulsion, praise; such a one, you, in the battle (वृत्रतूर्ये (*vṛtratūrye*)), for the destruction of the foe or for the destruction of sin, be aware (बोधि (*bodhi*)). Or else, be aware in the battle. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Agni) यं (*yaṃ*)—which
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- जनासः (*janāsaḥ*)—all the praisers
- सबाधः (*sabādhaḥ*)—gathered together
- वाजसातये (*vājasātaye*)—for the gain of food
- ईळते (*īḷate*)—praise
- सः (*saḥ*)—such you
- वृत्रतूर्ये (*vṛtratūrye*)—that in battle our enemies must be destroyed
- बोधि (*bodhi*)—know

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, you whom all the praisers, gathered together, praise for the gain of food, such as you, know that you must destroy our enemies in battle.

##### English translation
O you whom the crowding worshippers praise for the obtainment of food, attend to them for the destruction of their enemies.
